   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def refused_arm(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    """Refused means refused: a refused boot or a 403. A 404 means the request never' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    reached the guard and is never accepted as a refusal here."""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return (not booted(name)) or "RESET: 403" in arms.get(name, "")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def arms_starting(prefix):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return [n for n in arms if n.startswith(prefix)]' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'absent_arms = arms_starting("absent")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'prod_arms = arms_starting("production-plus-")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'test_arms = arms_starting("test-plus-")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'dev_arms = arms_starting("development-plus-")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# The echo claim: in a production-labelled arm, echo must stay off no matter which label' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# shaped flag is exported. The SQL_ECHO arm is the documented exception and is excluded.' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'echo_armed = [n for n in prod_arms' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '              if field(n, "MAIN_ECHO") == "True" or field(n, "SEED_ECHO") == "True"]' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'sql_echo_arm = "development-plus-sql_echo"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'clauses = [' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    ("p_absent_arm_no_reset",' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     bool(absent_arms) and all(not booted(n) or "RESET: 403" in arms[n] for n in absent_arms)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     and not any("RESET: 204" in arms[n] for n in absent_arms),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "with no ENV the wipe is never armed, with or without an invented opt-in flag: want a refused "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "boot or a 403 and never a 204; saw: %s" % (" || ".join(arms[n] for n in sorted(absent_arms))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                                                 or "ABSENT ARMS MISSING")),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    ("p_offlist_label_never_204",' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     bool(prod_arms) and all(refused_arm(n) for n in prod_arms)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     and not any("RESET: 204" in arms[n] for n in prod_arms),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "no second opt-in variable re-arms the reset under ENV=production (D-2 refused a second gate): "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "every production-plus-FLAG arm must refuse; saw: %s"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     % (" || ".join("%s=%s" % (n.split("production-plus-")[1], arms[n]) for n in sorted(prod_arms))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        or "PRODUCTION ARMS MISSING")),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    ("p_control_test_label_403",' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     [n for n in test_arms if n in arms] and all(refused_arm(n) for n in test_arms if n in arms),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "the test-labelled arms keep answering 403 with and without an opt-in flag, so the guard is "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "not merely broken; saw: %s"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     % (" || ".join("%s=%s" % (n, arms[n]) for n in sorted(test_arms) if n in arms)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        or "TEST ARMS MISSING")),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    ("p_explicit_development_204",' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     booted("development-bare") and "RESET: 204" in arms.get("development-bare", ""),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "with ENV=development and nothing else exported the documented reset still answers 204, so the "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "fix did not hide the dev convenience behind an undocumented flag; saw: %s"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     % (arms.get("development-bare") or "ARM MISSING")),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    ("p_only_sql_echo_arms_echo",' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     bool(prod_arms) and not echo_armed' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     and (field(sql_echo_arm, "MAIN_ECHO") == "True" or field(sql_echo_arm, "SEED_ECHO") == "True"),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "SQL_ECHO is the only exported variable that moves echo: no production arm may have an engine "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     "echoing (armed: %s) and the development SQL_ECHO arm must (saw: %s)"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '     % (",".join(sorted(echo_armed)) or "none", arms.get(sql_echo_arm) or "ARM MISSING")),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' ']' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'ok = True' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'details = []' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'for name, good, detail in clauses:' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if good:' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        details.append("PASS AC-11 %s" % name)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    else:' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        ok = False' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        details.append("FAIL AC-11 %s: %s" % (name, detail))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'if ok:' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("PASS AC-11: no second opt-in variable re-arms the reset or the echo in any named "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '          "environment, the test and production labels keep answering 403 with every flag exported, "' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '          "and the explicit-development reset needs no flag at all")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' else: >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("FAIL AC-11: at least one clause failed (see the FAIL lines below)")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("\n".join(details))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
test -s "$TMPDIR/clauses.py" || echo "FAIL: TMPDIR/clauses.py never reached disk"

sed -e "/sqlalchemy.engine/d" -e "/StarletteDeprecation/d" -e "/^  from starlette$/d" "$TMPDIR/out.txt"

[ "$RC" = 0 ] || echo "FAIL AC-11: the probe crashed rc=$RC (rc is diagnostic; the verdict token is the contract)"
[ "$RC2" = 0 ] || echo "FAIL AC-11: the clause table could not read the probe output"

    its two no-ENV arms quoted whole: `FAIL AC-11 p_absent_arm_no_reset: ... saw: BOOT ; HEALTH:
    {"status": "ok", "version": "0.1.0", "env": "development"} ; MAIN_ECHO: True ; SEED_ECHO: True
    ; RESET: 204 || BOOT ; HEALTH: {"status": "ok", "version": "0.1.0", "env": "development"} ;
    MAIN_ECHO: True ; SEED_ECHO: True ; RESET: 204` - one staff token, one unset variable, no
    second gate required, both arms wiped a seeded store (A-3 reproduced), and today's green pins
    `PASS AC-11 p_offlist_label_never_204` (`ARM production-plus-ALLOW_DATA_RESET | ...
    "env":"production" ; MAIN_ECHO: False ; SEED_ECHO: False ; RESET: 403`, and the same for the
    other six flag arms), `PASS AC-11 p_control_test_label_403`, `PASS AC-11
    p_explicit_development_204` and `PASS AC-11 p_only_sql_echo_arms_echo` (`ARM
    development-plus-sql_echo | ... MAIN_ECHO: True`, the only echo-moving export that works today
    beside the label coupling). Green-shape rehearsal, measured against a tree carrying D-1's
    required-`ENV` validator plus the decoupled `SQL_ECHO` default: `PASS AC-11: no second opt-in
    variable re-arms the reset or the echo in any named environment, ...` with all five clauses
    green, and the two absent arms reduced to `BOOT: ValidationError` with no `RESET:` line at
    all.

- [ ] **AC-12** `/health` keeps its `env` field and reports the named value **verbatim**, and the
reset guard's refusal does **not** depend on `/health` telling the truth: in a named-production
process the documented reset answers 403 and leaves the store fingerprint byte-identical, with or
without `SQL_ECHO` exported, and the same refusal and the same untouched store hold in a process
whose reported label is forced to `development` behind the guard's back.

Pass: the block prints a line beginning `PASS AC-12:`. Clauses: `p_prod_guard_refuses`,
`p_refusal_survives_echo_flag`, `p_health_field_verbatim`,
`p_refusal_independent_of_the_reported_label`, `p_mutant_guard_that_ignores_the_label_is_refused`.

D-4 keeps the field (a `test_health.py` assertion and an operator's only external read both need
it) and AC-3 already pins that the field tracks the variable. What AC-3 cannot pin is *independence*:
a tree whose guard consults the reported label rather than the resolved setting would answer 403
today in every arm AC-3 runs, and would answer 204 the moment an operator, a proxy or a future
refactor moved what `/health` reads. Round 1's Constraints shortfall 4 names exactly this: "a
fourth arm - a fix that repairs the guard but leaves `/health` reporting a defaulted value - was
reasoned about in D-4 and never built." This round builds it, twice, and says which half is
measured where.

Two arms, two halves of the mutant, and they are not symmetric:

- **The injected half, measured live.** A child process boots with `ENV=production`, and after the
  import completes an audit hook rebinds the module-level `settings` object that the `/health`
  handler reads to one whose `env` is `development` - `app.config` is never patched, the guard
  still reads the real validated setting, and the arm prints a `PUBLISHED_ENV: development` banner
  so it cannot be mistaken for a real development boot. `/health` then reports `development` while
  the guard must still answer 403 and still touch nothing. If the guard derived anything from what
  the process *reports*, this is the arm that shows it.
- **The mutated-tree half, also measured live.** The block runs
  `python3 _docs/issues/_t20/ac12_mutation.py <scratch>` (committed), which copies the tree under
  test to a scratch directory, leaves `config.py` and `database.py` exactly as found, and rewrites
  one line - the guard's condition becomes `if False:`, i.e. the env term is dropped and the
  endpoint honours the documented confirm in *every* named environment. The mutation step asserts
  its own delta and prints `MUTATION_LINES`, so the arm cannot be built wrong silently. The clause
  then requires the asymmetry: the mutant's documented confirm must MOVE the store fingerprint
  (`204` plus a new `max(updated_at)`), while a differently-cased confirm stays refused by the
  contract's `Literal` and leaves it alone. That pair is what tells "a guard that reads the named
  environment" apart from "an endpoint that never answers", and it is the reason the three
  refusal-clause PASSes above cannot be bought by disabling the route. If the mutation tree cannot
  be built or cannot boot, the arm prints `ARM NOT RUN` / an import-time refusal, and the clause
  reports that honestly instead of scoring it - it never silently converts to green.

Honest scope marker (an UNMEASURABLE-here in this issue's own idiom): the *inverse* mutant - guard
genuinely fixed while `/health` alone defaults - cannot be built as a tree mutation that this
block is allowed to score, because the only production code path that makes `/health` lie without
also touching what the guard reads is a hard-coded literal in `main.py`, and a hard-coded
`"env": "development"` is refused by AC-3's `p_dev_reports_dev`/`p_test_reports_test` control arms.
The independence claim is therefore measured here by the injected rebind (which is the same
observable state, reached without editing the reviewed tree), and the *hard-coded-literal* version
is covered by AC-3 rather than duplicated here. That division is stated rather than hidden, and it
is why this AC's PASS line names both the field and the independence.

   printf '%s\n' 'from pathlib import Path' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# The arm line delimiter, assembled from a character rather than spelled: a literal pipe' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# may not appear in a staged probe, because the tools that slice blocks out of the issue' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# pair fence markers and split arm lines on that character. probe9.py checks that the bytes' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# a block stages are exactly these bytes, so the check has to deny itself too.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'V = chr(124)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'OUT = HERE / "run"' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'DB = (OUT / "ac12.db").resolve()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'CHILD = r"""' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import json' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import os' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import sys' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import time' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = "sqlite:///" + os.environ["T20DB"]' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' try: >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    from app.main import app' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'except Exception as exc:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("BOOT:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    raise SystemExit(0)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def published_label(value):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    import app.config' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    class _Label:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        def __init__(self, v):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '            self.env = v' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        def __getattr__(self, name):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '            return getattr(app.config.get_settings(), name)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return _Label(value)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'if os.environ.get("T20_AC12_PUBLISH"):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # The /health handler reads the module global at call time, so rebinding it after the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # import completes is the honest injection point: app.config is never patched, the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # reset guard keeps reading the real validated setting, and the hook prints a banner' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # naming the label it published so this arm cannot be mistaken for a real dev boot.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    sys.modules["app.main"].settings = published_label(os.environ["T20_AC12_PUBLISH"])' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    sys.modules["app.main"]._t20_ac12_published = True' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("PUBLISHED_ENV:", os.environ["T20_AC12_PUBLISH"], flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("BOOT", flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'from fastapi.testclient import TestClient' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import sqlalchemy as sa' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'from app.database import SessionLocal, engine' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'from jose import jwt' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'c = TestClient(app)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'r = c.get("/health")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("HEALTHCODE:", r.status_code, flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("HEALTHBODY:", json.dumps(r.json()), flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'tok = jwt.encode({"sub": "staff", "role": "staff", "iat": int(time.time()),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                  "exp": int(time.time()) + 3600},' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                 os.environ["JWT_SECRET"], algorithm="HS256")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def cell(conn, sql):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    try:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        return str(conn.execute(sa.text(sql)).scalar())' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    except Exception:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        return "ERR"' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def fingerprint(conn):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return "%s/%s/%s" % (' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        cell(conn, "select count(*) from waitlist_entries"),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        (cell(conn, "select staff_pin_hash from settings") or "none")[:7],' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        cell(conn, "select max(updated_at) from waitlist_entries"))' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'with engine.begin() as conn:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("COUNT_BEFORE:", fingerprint(conn), flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'CONFIRM = os.environ.get("T20_CONFIRM", "RESET")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("CONFIRM:", CONFIRM, flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'r = c.post("/api/v1/admin/reset", json={"confirm": CONFIRM},' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '           headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + tok})' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'print("RESET:", r.status_code, flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'with engine.begin() as conn:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print("COUNT_AFTER:", fingerprint(conn), flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'SessionLocal.remove()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '"""' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'CHILD_FILE = OUT / "child12.py"' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'CHILD_FILE.write_text(CHILD)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def base_env():' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return {' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin"),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "HOME": os.path.expanduser("~"),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "TZ": "UTC",' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "TMPDIR": str(OUT),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "PYTHONPATH": os.environ["T20PROBEPATH"],' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "JWT_SECRET": os.environ.get("JWT_SECRET", "t20groom-ac12a" + "a" * 33),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "STAFF_PIN": "0000",' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "DATABASE_URL": "sqlite:///" + str(DB),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "T20DB": str(DB),' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        "ENV": "production",' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    }' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def tree_env(label):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    """One arm is a MUTATION arm: it runs against a copy of the tree under test carried in' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    T20_AC12_MUTATION_TREE, where the reset guard'"'"'s condition has been rewritten (see the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    arm list). The tree copy exists so the mutation is visible in the diff of a scratch tree' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    rather than in the tree under review.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    """' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return base_env()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def run(label, extra_env, mutation=False, confirm="RESET"):' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if DB.exists():' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        DB.unlink()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    env = tree_env(label)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    env.update(extra_env)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if mutation:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        target = os.environ.get("T20_AC12_MUTATION_TREE")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        if not target:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '            print("ARM %s " + V + " ARM NOT RUN: T20_AC12_MUTATION_TREE is unset, so the "' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                  "mutation arm has no tree to measure (it is built by the block'"'"'s own "' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                  "mutation step, never by hand)" % label, flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '            return' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        env["PYTHONPATH"] = str(Path(target) / "backend")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        env["T20DB"] = str(DB)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        env["DATABASE_URL"] = "sqlite:///" + str(DB)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # The store is seeded once per arm under an explicit development boot (the documented' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    # reset contract), so the fingerprint has real rows and a real stored PIN hash to move.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    seed = subprocess.run(' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        [sys.executable, "-c", "from app.seed import seed_data; seed_data(reset=True)"],' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        env=dict(env, ENV="development"), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    env["T20_CONFIRM"] = confirm' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if mutation:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # The mutation tree is a copy of the tree under test, so its child must run with the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # copy'"'"'s own module path AND its own document tree: the child file itself is re-staged' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # into the copy so __file__-relative lookups cannot reach the reviewed tree.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        mut_child = Path(env["PYTHONPATH"]).parent / "_t20_ac12_child.py"' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        mut_child.write_text(CHILD, encoding="utf-8")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        child = mut_child' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    else:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        child = CHILD_FILE' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(child)], env=env,' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    obs = [ln for ln in res.stdout.splitlines()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '           if ln.startswith(("BOOT", "HEALTHCODE:", "HEALTHBODY:", "RESET:", "CONFIRM:",' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                             "COUNT_", "PUBLISHED_ENV:"))]' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if not obs:' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # An arm that printed nothing at all is a harness failure, not a behaviour, so the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # last line the child managed to write is carried into the ARM line. The delimiter is' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        # substituted so the ARM line stays one parseable record.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        tail = [ln for ln in (res.stderr + res.stdout).splitlines() if ln.strip()]' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        obs = ["NO VERDICT LINE tail=" + (tail[-1][:160] if tail else "nothing")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '               + " seedrc=" + str(seed.returncode)]' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    summary = " ; ".join(obs)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    print(("ARM %s " + V + " %s") % (label, summary), flush=True)' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'run("prod-bare", {})' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'run("prod-echo-requested", {"SQL_ECHO": "true"})' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'run("prod-health-defaults-development", {"T20_AC12_PUBLISH": "development"})' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# The second half of the fourth mutant: a tree whose guard answers 403 for EVERY named' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# environment, i.e. the fail-open is closed only by disabling the endpoint. The store' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# fingerprint is what refuses it: the request that the fixed tree refuses here, this tree' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# wipes, so the two arms cannot be confused by a status-code-only reader.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'run("mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-documented-confirm", {}, True, confirm="RESET")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# Same mutation tree, differently-cased confirm: recorded so the reader can see that the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# mutant honours the documented confirm in a named-production process at all, which is the' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# whole refusal of this clause. The fix cannot reach this arm'"'"'s answer under any confirm.' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'run("mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-alternate-confirm", {}, True, confirm="Development")' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'if DB.exists():' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    DB.unlink()' >> "$TMPDIR/probe.py"
test -s "$TMPDIR/probe.py" || echo "FAIL: TMPDIR/probe.py never reached disk"
first=:
   printf '%s\n' '"""T20 AC-12 clause table: the 403 is not conditional on /health'"'"'s honesty.' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'Four clauses. The first two are the fix-shape pins (a named-production process answers 403 and' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'rewrites nothing, with or without the documented echo flag). The third is this AC'"'"'s own red at' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'the groom base, where no ENV-independent guard exists to be independent of anything. The fourth' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'is the mutant-arm clause: with an audit hook publishing "development" through the object the' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '/health handler reads, the guard must still refuse and still touch nothing - the refusal must' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'not be a side effect of the label the process happens to report.' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '"""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'import sys' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# The arm line delimiter, assembled from a character rather than spelled: a literal pipe' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# may not appear in a staged probe, because the tools that slice blocks out of the issue' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# pair fence markers and split arm lines on that character.' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'V = chr(124)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'lines = open(sys.argv[1]).read() if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] else ""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'arms = {}' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'for ln in lines.splitlines():' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    if ln.startswith("ARM "):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        name, _, obs = ln[4:].partition(" " + V + " ")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        arms[name.strip()] = obs' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def field(name, key):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    for chunk in arms.get(name, "").split(" ; "):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '        if chunk.startswith(key + ":"):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '            return chunk.split(":", 1)[1].strip()' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return ""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def booted(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return any(c.strip() == "BOOT" for c in arms.get(name, "").split(" ; "))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def wiped(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    """True: the two store fingerprints differ, i.e. the reset really ran."""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return untouched(name) is True' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# The block stages two arms against the mutation tree, named by the confirm they send. The' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# mutation drops the env term from the guard'"'"'s condition, so the DOCUMENTED confirm is' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# honoured in a named-production process (204 and a moved fingerprint) while the' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# differently-cased one is still refused by the contract'"'"'s Literal (422, store unmoved) -' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# exactly as in the fix. That pair is the signature: any arm that answers 403 to the' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# documented confirm, or leaves the store untouched there, is not the mutant the clause is' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '# written against, and saying so beats reporting a green built on the wrong delta.' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'MUTANT_DOC = "mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-documented-confirm"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'MUTANT_ALT = "mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-alternate-confirm"' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'HARNESS_LIMITS = ("ARM NOT RUN", "MUTATION TREE UNREADABLE", "BOOT:")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def harness_limited(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return any(m in arms.get(name, "") for m in HARNESS_LIMITS)' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def describe_mutants():' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return " || ".join("%s=%s" % (n.replace("mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-", ""),' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '                                  arms.get(n) or "ARM MISSING") for n in (MUTANT_DOC, MUTANT_ALT))' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def json_of(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    """The HEALTHBODY chunk is the response body, printed on its own line precisely because a' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    JSON body is full of colons: chunk splitting here is on " ; " and key matching on the first' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    colon, so a payload with its own punctuation needs its own label."""' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return field(name, "HEALTHBODY")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def health_status(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '    return field(name, "HEALTHCODE")' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' '' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"
   printf '%s\n' 'def health_env(name):' >> "$TMPDIR/clauses.py"

    Measured today, two measurements because this block's verdict is about the FIX rather than
    about the defect, and both are printed rather than one of them: (a) At base `bad4f98`, where
    the guard is the original string equality against a defaulted field, the block prints `PASS
    AC-12: /health keeps its env field and reports the named value verbatim, and the reset guard's
    403 and untouched store hold even when the reported label is forced to development behind the
    guard's back` with all five clauses green (`ARM prod-bare | BOOT ; HEALTHCODE: 200 ;
    HEALTHBODY: {"status": "ok", "version": "0.1.0", "env": "production"} ; COUNT_BEFORE:
    9/$2b$12$/2026-09-13 21:10:20.265862 ; RESET: 403 ; COUNT_AFTER: <byte-identical>`, `ARM
    prod-health-defaults-development | PUBLISHED_ENV: development ; ... RESET: 403`, and `ARM
    mutant_guard-ignores-the-named-environment-documented-confirm | ... RESET: 204 ; COUNT_AFTER:
    <moved>` against `MUTATION MUTATION_LINES: 2 (- if get_settings().env != "development": ; + if
    False: # AC-12 MUTATION: the guard no longer reads the named environment)`). AC-12 is a
    **not-breaking pin at base**, and it is recorded as green-on-purpose: the base tree's guard
    does read `get_settings().env`, so D-1's own blast radius - not any existing defect - is what
    this block could break. The defect AC-12 watches for is introduced, not inherited, and a block
    that only watches for introduced damage is worth having only if its red baseline says so. What
    AC-12 measures red at base is visible in the same output and belongs to other blocks: the
    no-ENV wipe (AC-1/AC-2) and the development echo coupling (AC-5), neither of which this block
    re-books. (b) Against a tree carrying D-1's required-`ENV` validator and the decoupled
    `SQL_ECHO` default, the same block prints the same `PASS AC-12:` line with all five clauses
    green, i.e. the fix does not break the independence it is supposed to preserve. (c) The
    mutation arm's discriminating power, measured rather than asserted: against that same
    fix-shaped tree with only the guard line mutated (`_docs/issues/_t20/ac12_mutation.py`), the
    block prints `FAIL AC-12: at least one clause failed (see the FAIL lines below)` naming
    `p_prod_guard_refuses` and `p_refusal_independent_of_the_reported_label`, because the mutated
    guard answers `RESET: 204` and moves the fingerprint in a named-production process; and
    against a tree whose `/health` literal is hard-coded to `development`, this block's
    `p_health_field_verbatim` reddens too. The three states - fix, guard mutant, health mutant -
    therefore produce three different clause sets, which is the property a mutation arm is
    supposed to have. hi

## Test requirements

What the implementation round adds to `backend/tests/`, and what it must not move. The blocks above
measure the shipped behaviour from outside; a unit test is what keeps it there, and the two jobs are
different, so both are required.

- **Add `backend/tests/test_config_env.py`** (new file, so a later round can find the pin): D-1's
  refusal is a construction-time fact, so it is tested at construction, not through HTTP. Required
  clauses, each a separate test so a failure names itself: `Settings()` with `ENV` absent raises a
  pydantic `ValidationError` whose message names `env`; `ENV=""` raises the same way (an empty
  string is a value, and D-1 refuses it); `ENV=staging` raises with the allow-list quoted; and each
  of `development`/`test`/`production` constructs with `settings.env` equal to exactly that value.
  Add one test for `sql_echo` defaulting to `False` in all three labels, and one constructing with
  `SQL_ECHO=true` under `ENV=production`, because D-3's whole claim is that the two fields are
  independent - the pair of them is the test, neither alone is.
- **Add `test_admin_reset.py` cases, do not rewrite that file**: `test` and `production` answer 403
  and leave the row counts untouched (AC-2's controls), and the 403 body carries the documented
  error code rather than a bare 403. Keep `:143`'s existing "the endpoint reads the resolved
  setting" case exactly as it is - D-2 chose that seam and that test is what pins it.
- **Do not touch `test_health.py`'s `env`-presence assertion** (D-4). Extend it to assert the value
  equals the `ENV` the process was given. Under D-1 there is no implicit value left to report, so a
  test that only checks the field exists would pass on the broken tree.
- **Repair, do not delete, the modules AC-4 measures red.** `backend/tests/test_seed.py` and any
  other module that constructs settings without stating an environment must state one above its
  imports (the shape `conftest.py:16` already uses). Deleting or `skip`-ing a module turns AC-4's
  measured red into a fake green and will be caught by that block's own clause list.
- **Standing floors.** The whole-suite count at this base is **272 passed / 2 xfailed / 0 failed**
  (measured; Groom baseline item 4). That 272 is the inherited pre-T10 number: T10's own AC-6
  standing floor is **286 passed / 2 xfailed** once its suite additions are merged, and this
  worktree's tree does not carry them yet. Read the floor as max(286 + this issue's additions, the
  measured whole-suite count of the branch you land on), never as a fixed 274. This issue's own
  additions are **at least 6 items** (the refusal / empty / off-list / three-allowed-values /
  `sql_echo`-default / explicit-`SQL_ECHO` tests above), so on a branch that already carries T10's
  work the DoD floor is **292 items, 0 failed, 0 errors** (286 + 6, never a literal 274). The count is
  the cheap check that nothing
  was deleted to buy a green; it is not a substitute for the blocks. `backend/tests/_atomicity_probe.py`
  stays outside the suite (AC-4 runs it as its own arm; it is a probe, not a test).
- **Echo must be asserted on both engines**, `app/database.py` and `app/seed.py`, from the objects
  themselves (`engine.echo`) rather than from a settings field: the field can be correct while the
  `create_engine(echo=...)` argument is not, which is precisely AC-5's second clause.

## Implementation notes

- `backend/app/config.py`: add `env: Literal["development", "test", "production"]` with **no**
  default, plus a `sql_echo: bool = False` field. D-1's presence check has to fire at construction:
  `model_fields_set` is empty of `env` when the variable is absent (measured, Groom baseline 1), and
  a `@model_validator(mode="after")` reading `self.model_fields_set` is the shape that works; a
  `field_validator` cannot see the field it is validating in `info.data`. A companion
  `env_explicit: bool` attribute is explicitly *not* the mechanism and no test should require one.
- `backend/app/database.py:42` and `backend/app/seed.py:35`: replace `echo=settings.env ==
  "development"` with `echo=settings.sql_echo`. Two call sites, no other change. Do not add a
  `log_config`, a handler, or a level - that is T21 (D-3).
- `backend/app/main.py`: the reset guard keeps `get_settings().env == "development"`; under D-1 the
  allow-list makes the comparison total, so no extra `in` check is needed and adding one would
  re-introduce a second place where an unexpected value could pass.
- `.env` is still consulted by pydantic-settings (`env_file=".env"`). A `.env` supplying `ENV`
  satisfies the requirement, which is intended - it is a stated choice, not a default - but no
  `.env` file may be added to the repo, and `README.md:275` must stop describing `ENV` as optional
  with default `development` in the same commit as the code.
- Sequencing: config first (everything else depends on the field existing), then the two echo
  sites, then the guard, then the test-harness repair last, because AC-4 is the block that measures
  the harness and repairing it early hides the red that proves the other three fixes landed.

## Dependencies

- **T10 is merged and load-bearing here** (base `1fc1521`): its secret-quality gate means a short or
  published `JWT_SECRET` no longer boots, so every block and every new test must supply a
  gate-compliant secret or it measures a refusal that has nothing to do with `ENV`. The blocks do;
  new tests should follow `conftest.py`, which already carries one.
- **`pydantic-settings` v2 semantics** (D-1's measured note): `os.environ` and constructor kwargs
  fold into one validated namespace, which is why presence at construction is the only signal. If
  the dependency ever moves to v1-style sources, D-1's mechanism has to be revisited, not just
  re-tested.
- **`backend/tests/conftest.py`** is the harness's environment statement (`:16`) and therefore an
  input to AC-4, not merely a test file. Any change to how it states variables re-measures AC-4.
- **`README.md:275`** documents `ENV` as optional with default `development`. That documentation is
  part of the defect surface (A-1 names the docs) and part of the fix's contract change.

## Out of scope

- **The logger half of the echo story - T21.** Handler, formatter, level, uvicorn's duplicate ASGI
  traceback, traceback verbosity, and the `[SQL: ...]`-inside-an-exception-message leak (D-3). That
  leak travels the logging path and survives any `echo=` change, so it is not this issue's fix and
  no AC here claims it. T20 owns the default coupling between `env` and `echo` and nothing else
  about logging.
- **The seed-literal half of the destructive surface - T23 (#78).** AC-2 stops the wipe from being
  armed by an unset variable; it does not touch what the wipe does when legitimately armed, nor the
  shipped seed credentials.
- **A second opt-in gate for the reset** (`ALLOW_DATA_RESET` or similar). D-2 rejected it: a second
  flag whose default is also a default is the same class of bug one level down.
- **Trimming `/health` to `{"status":"ok"}`** (A-11's proposal). D-4 keeps the `env` field; AC-3
  pins that it reports what was chosen.
- **`default="production"`** as an alternative to D-1, and any other silent default in either
  direction. Rejected with reasons in D-1; a block that passes on a flipped default is exactly the
  mutant AC-1's control arm is written to reject.
- **Non-backend consumers of `ENV`** - the frontend build, `docker-compose`, deployment examples. The
  contract change is real for them (Constraints) but changing their run commands is a separate
  mechanical issue; nothing in the audit's A-3/D-2 exposure depends on them.

## Constraints

- Files this fix may touch: `backend/app/config.py`, `backend/app/database.py`, `backend/app/seed.py`,
  `backend/app/main.py`, `backend/tests/` (repair + new tests), `README.md` (the `ENV` row),
  `_docs/deployment.md` (the `ENV` row and the `Set ENV=production` instruction, both pinned by
  AC-10), `_docs/plan.md` where it describes the reset endpoint's guard and the `ENV` label's
  meaning, and `_docs/issues/_t20/` (the probe sources, the generator, the recorder, the digest
  checker, and AC-12's committed mutation builder). Nothing under `_docs/openapi.yaml` changes:
  no new field, endpoint or response code - a 403 on a route that already answers 403 is not a
  schema change.
- Every block must be run one at a time and never under `set -e` / `set -o pipefail`, and never
  piped into a truncating consumer: the verdict is the printed token, and `rc` is diagnostic only.

## Definition of Done

- AC-1, AC-2, AC-3, AC-5 each print their `PASS AC-<n>:` verdict line against the fixed tree, from
  the repo root, on a tree where `git status --porcelain` was empty before the run, and each still
  prints at least one `FAIL` line if any of its clauses is falsified. The `Measured today:` red
  baselines stay in the file as the record of what these blocks measured *before* the fix; they are
  not to be updated to green.
- AC-4 prints `PASS AC-4:` for every module it names, including the one that is red today, and the
  whole backend suite is green in an environment that supplies the gate variables and never names
  `ENV` - which is the same statement as "the harness states its own environment", and it is the
  clause that catches a fix that satisfies every pin and leaves the tests unrunnable.
- AC-10 prints `PASS AC-10:` against the fixed tree: both documents' `ENV` rows read required with
  no permissive default and name all three allowed values, the bare `Set ENV=production`
  instruction is gone, and no line in either file still calls `ENV` optional. The three red
  `Measured today:` lines above stay red in the file.
- AC-11 prints `PASS AC-11:` against the fixed tree, and its `p_explicit_development_204` clause is
  the one a fix must not "pass" by adding a flag: if the documented development reset needs an
  export that `README.md` does not name, the block is red and so is the fix.
- AC-12 prints `PASS AC-12:` against the fixed tree *and* against the two mutation states its text
  names (guard mutated, `/health` literal mutated), producing three different clause sets. A round
  that reports `PASS AC-12:` without having seen the mutation arm's `RESET: 204` and moved
  fingerprint has not measured the clause it is claiming.
- AC-13 prints `PASS AC-13:` against the landed file, i.e. the digest table and the committed probe
  sources are in sync with the blocks that ship, the staged block body equals the issue's region,
  and the checker reports zero drift. Because it is a meta criterion, AC-13 is expected to be
  GREEN at the fix commit and RED if anyone hand-edits a block: that inversion is deliberate and
  it is the reason shortfall 5 is closed.
- Whole-suite floor: **0 failed, 0 errors**, and a collected count of at least **292** on a branch
  that carries T10's suite additions (286 standing floor + this issue's >=6), or at least **278** on
  a base that still measures 272 - re-measure the whole suite at the landing commit and record the
  number, because a fixed floor quoted from a different branch is the kind of number that gets a
  green from a deletion. Neither 292 nor 278 is a promise about a branch; both are this issue's own
  additions measured against a base this round could actually run. `test_seed.py` and `test_config.py` still exist and still run (they are the
  two a shortcut would delete rather than repair).
- `README.md` no longer documents `ENV` as optional, and neither does `_docs/deployment.md` - both
  are machine-checked by AC-10 rather than asserted here.
- `_docs/plan.md` gets a note wherever it states the reset endpoint's guard condition or what the
  `ENV` label drives, so a later round does not "restore" the `env == "development"` echo coupling
  from plan text.
- **Ten-block run record (groom round 2).** The shared runner (`runacs.py`) was pointed at this file
  exactly once at the end of round 2, from the repo root, `--timeout 900`. Expected block count is
  **ten**: AC-1..AC-5 (round 1, gate-1 measured `green=0 red=5` at `a254e49`, not re-verified by
  this round because the gate already did it) plus AC-10, AC-11, AC-12, AC-13 (round 2). The split
  this round measured per block, one block at a time, each in its own fresh copy of the tree so no
  block's scratch or mutation directory was visible to another: AC-10 `FAIL` at three clauses,
  AC-11 `FAIL` at one clause and four green, AC-12 `PASS` as a declared not-breaking pin (with its
  mutation arm's red measured separately against a mutated tree), AC-13 recorded at the commit that
  lands its own digest table. Any `green=1 red=3` style split that a later gate reports for the
  four round-2 blocks must be reconcilable with those four lines, and any *silent* block in that
  run is a harness finding rather than a result.
- No block under this issue was edited to buy a green. If a fix legitimately requires changing what
  a block stages, the change goes through `generate_t20_probes.py` and `record_t20_digests.py` in
  the same commit, and the commit message says which clause the fix made untrue and why. AC-13 is
  the machine check that makes skipping that step visible instead of merely improper.
